{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"gen-1783068551-1aMjts7HieUMoX3wxFkT","type":"message","role":"assistant","container":null,"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"Write_12","caller":{"type":"direct"},"name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/update_playbook.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nHWPX Renewal Playbook Updater\n\nThis script updates the renewal_playbook.hwpx file with new values from:\n- renewal_update.json: customer, owner, renewal window, pricing band, escalation, note\n- followups.csv: follow-up items (sorted by sequence)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport tempfile\nimport zipfile\nimport xml.etree.ElementTree as ET\n\n\ndef register_namespaces():\n    \"\"\"Register all namespaces to prevent ns0:, ns1: prefix corruption.\"\"\"\n    # From section XML files\n    ET.register_namespace('hp', 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML')\n    ET.register_namespace('opf', 'http://www.idpf.org/2007/opf')\n\n\ndef parse_renewal_update(json_path):\n    \"\"\"Parse renewal_update.json and create old->new mapping.\"\"\"\n    with open(json_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    # Based on document analysis, these are the old->new mappings\n    # Old values found in section0.xml and section1.xml\n    mappings = {\n        # Customer name (split across two text elements in the original)\n        'Northwind ': 'Asteron ',  # First part\n        'Retail': 'Commerce',      # Second part\n\n        # Current owner\n        '이전담당자': data['owner_name'],\n\n        # Renewal window - update both occurrences\n        '2025-04-01 ~ 2025-04-15': f\"{data['window_start']} ~ {data['window_end']}\",\n\n        # Pricing band\n        'Standard-18M': data['band'],\n\n        # Escalation contact\n        'ops-old@northwind.example': data['escalation_email'],\n\n        # Pricing note\n        '기존 메모를 새 메모로 바꾸세요.': data['note'],\n    }\n\n    return mappings, data\n\n\ndef parse_followups(csv_path):\n    \"\"\"Parse followups.csv and return items sorted by sequence.\"\"\"\n    items = []\n    with open(csv_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        for row in reader:\n            items.append({\n                'sequence': int(row['sequence']),\n                'item': row['item']\n            })\n\n    # Sort by sequence\n    items.sort(key=lambda x: x['sequence'])\n    return items\n\n\ndef get_namespace_prefixes():\n    \"\"\"Get namespace prefixes for ElementTree.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        'hp': 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML',\n        'opf': 'http://www.idpf.org/2007/opf'\n    }\n\n\ndef extract_hwpx(hwpx_path, extract_dir):\n    \"\"\"Extract HWPX file to a directory.\"\"\"\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(hwpx_path, 'r') as z:\n        z.extractall(extract_dir)\n\n\ndef find_text_elements(root, ns):\n    \"\"\"Find all text-bearing 't' elements in the document.\"\"\"\n    text_elements = []\n    hp_ns = ns['hp']\n    t_tag = f'{{{hp_ns}}}t'\n\n    for elem in root.iter():\n        if elem.tag == t_tag and elem.text is not None:\n            text_elements.append(elem)\n\n    return text_elements\n\n\ndef find_parent_paragraph(element, ns):\n    \"\"\"Find the parent paragraph (p element) of a text element.\"\"\"\n    hp_ns = ns['hp']\n    p_tag = f'{{{hp_ns}}}p'\n\n    parent = element\n    while parent is not None:\n        if parent.tag == p_tag:\n            return parent\n        parent = parent.getparent() if hasattr(parent, 'getparent') else None\n    return None\n\n\ndef get_full_text_of_paragraph(p_elem, ns):\n    \"\"\"Get the full concatenated text of a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    hp_ns = ns['hp']\n    t_tag = f'{{{hp_ns}}}t'\n\n    texts = []\n    for t_elem in p_elem.iter(t_tag):\n        if t_elem.text:\n            texts.append(t_elem.text)\n    return ''.join(texts)\n\n\ndef remove_lineSegArray_from_paragraph(p_elem, ns):\n    \"\"\"Remove lineSegArray elements from a paragraph to force re-rendering.\"\"\"\n    hp_ns = ns['hp']\n    lineSegArray_tag = f'{{{hp_ns}}}linesegarray'\n\n    # Find and remove all lineSegArray children\n    to_remove = []\n    for child in p_elem:\n        if child.tag == lineSegArray_tag:\n            to_remove.append(child)\n\n    for child in to_remove:\n        p_elem.remove(child)\n\n\ndef process_section_xml(section_path, field_mappings, followup_mappings, preserve_text, ns):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a section XML file, applying text replacements.\n\n    Args:\n        section_path: Path to the section XML file\n        field_mappings: Dict of old->new values for fields\n        followup_mappings: Dict of old->new values for follow-ups\n        preserve_text: Text that should not be modified (appendix preservation)\n        ns: Namespace dict\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (modified: bool, tree: ElementTree)\n    \"\"\"\n    tree = ET.parse(section_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    hp_ns = ns['hp']\n    p_tag = f'{{{hp_ns}}}p'\n    t_tag = f'{{{hp_ns}}}t'\n\n    modified = False\n    modified_paragraphs = set()  # Track which paragraph IDs were modified\n\n    # First pass: identify paragraphs to preserve\n    preserve_paragraph_ids = set()\n    for p_elem in root.iter(p_tag):\n        para_text = get_full_text_of_paragraph(p_elem, ns)\n        if preserve_text in para_text:\n            para_id = p_elem.get('id')\n            if para_id:\n                preserve_paragraph_ids.add(para_id)\n\n    # Second pass: apply replacements\n    for p_elem in root.iter(p_tag):\n        para_id = p_elem.get('id')\n\n        # Skip paragraphs that contain the preservation text\n        if para_id in preserve_paragraph_ids:\n            continue\n\n        para_modified = False\n\n        # Get all text elements in this paragraph\n        for t_elem in p_elem.iter(t_tag):\n            if t_elem.text is None:\n                continue\n\n            original_text = t_elem.text\n            new_text = original_text\n\n            # Apply field mappings\n            for old_val, new_val in field_mappings.items():\n                if old_val in new_text:\n                    new_text = new_text.replace(old_val, new_val)\n\n            # Apply follow-up mappings\n            for old_val, new_val in followup_mappings.items():\n                if old_val in new_text:\n                    new_text = new_text.replace(old_val, new_val)\n\n            if new_text != original_text:\n                t_elem.text = new_text\n                para_modified = True\n\n        if para_modified:\n            modified_paragraphs.add(para_id)\n            modified = True\n\n    # Third pass: remove lineSegArray from modified paragraphs\n    for p_elem in root.iter(p_tag):\n        para_id = p_elem.get('id')\n        if para_id in modified_paragraphs:\n            remove_lineSegArray_from_paragraph(p_elem, ns)\n\n    return modified, tree\n\n\ndef create_followup_mappings(followup_items, old_followups):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create mappings from old follow-up text to new follow-up text.\n\n    Args:\n        followup_items: List of follow-up items sorted by sequence\n        old_followups: List of old follow-up texts in document order\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict mapping old follow-up text to new follow-up text\n    \"\"\"\n    mappings = {}\n\n    # Map old follow-ups to new ones in order\n    for i, old_text in enumerate(old_followups):\n        if i < len(followup_items):\n            new_item = followup_items[i]\n            new_text = f\"{new_item['sequence']}. {new_item['item']}\"\n            mappings[old_text] = new_text\n\n    return mappings\n\n\ndef repackage_hwpx(source_dir, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Repackage the HWPX file with proper compression settings.\"\"\"\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(source_dir):\n            for file in files:\n                file_path = os.path.join(root, file)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(file_path, source_dir)\n\n                # Check if this is the mimetype file (should be stored, not compressed)\n                if arcname == 'mimetype':\n                    zf.write(file_path, arcname, compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_STORED)\n                else:\n                    zf.write(file_path, arcname, compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED)\n\n\ndef validate_output(hwpx_path, expected_values, followup_items, preserve_text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate the output HWPX file.\n\n    Args:\n        hwpx_path: Path to the updated HWPX file\n        expected_values: Dict of expected new values\n        followup_items: List of follow-up items\n        preserve_text: Text that should be preserved\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (success: bool, errors: list)\n    \"\"\"\n    errors = []\n\n    # Apply field mappings\n            for old_val, new_val in field_mappings.items():\n                if old_val in new_text:\n                    new_text = new_text.replace(old_val, new_val)\n\n            #
